# Public web version: published files

Built by `publish/build_public.py` from `tool/` (13 Nov 2026). 1344 files, 222.8 MB. Served by GitHub Pages from an Actions artifact: none of these data files is committed to git.

## The leak check (the build fails on any of these)

- Any PDF; any file from `checks/` or `sources/library/private/`.
- Files derived from licensed lot data (local only): Edmonton `l_` tiles, Edmonton hub boards and growth data, and Edmonton test fits other than PNG.
- Any licensed lot outline: 300,327 ring fingerprints (the first six encoded numbers of every outline) searched in every text file.
- Any Edmonton board outline: three or more consecutive corners of a board (33 corners, 7 boards); tile outlines are decoded to positions first. One- or two-corner matches are listed below (goal: none).
- Any point or address from the licensed assessment list: 679 addresses searched as whole words; addresses a public record also carries are listed below.
- Internal names: the project’s note folder, people’s initials and working-session names, and the licensed list’s source key.
- `index.html` without the public flag (Edmonton cards would not switch to the public text).
- Every stripped script must still parse (`node --check`).

## Result

- Problems: 0. One- or two-corner board matches: 0. Single trivial corners like (0, 0) not listed: 1847.
- licensed property records (local only) addresses also carried by a public record (kept): 111 5Th Ave E, 175 Cordova St W. Both are data-centre addresses the operators publish (the data-centre inventory, data/facilities.json).

## Left out as doubtful (the author’s rule: leave out, list, don’t ask)

- District-energy service areas (receiver "nets", 7 outlines): traced from City maps, a budget PDF and a utility company’s web page; none is under an open licence.
- Alberta post-secondary campuses (receiver source abPSI, 1 point): from a PDF list on alberta.ca with no stated open licence.
- The typology specification copies (`f1/specs/`): written in the project’s working notes.
- Fonts the tool no longer uses (Open Sans, Barlow).
- `hero_preview.html`: no named source and licence in the build’s table
- `context/edm_structures.json`: no named source and licence in the build’s table

## Text changes in the published copy

- Comments removed from every script, stylesheet and the page.
- In the remaining text, internal names are rewritten: initials and dates of decisions → "(project decision, date"; working-session names → "the project" or "the test-fit study"; note-folder paths → "project files"; in scripts only, the licensed list’s source key → `bcaList` (no such data is published).
